# Physics course of the year 2 of the natural sciences course of the University of Cambridge

↑ **Parent:** [Year 2 of the natural sciences course of the University of Cambridge](year-2-of-the-natural-sciences-course-of-the-university-of-cambridge.md)

Some Part IB Physics A and Physics B past papers have solutions alongside them in the restricted teaching system; the specific years have not been verified. Check each paper before selecting it for new solutions, and skip papers with existing solutions.[Easter 2026 holiday work, IB Physics A and IB Physics B: availability of past-paper solutions on the Cavendish website](https://www.crataegus.me.uk/supervisions/)
